# Traditional Industries & Local Businesses

[← back to index](../README.md) · 478 entries in this file · 72 categories in total

478 open-source projects in 72 categories. Every repo was confirmed on GitHub (2026-11-11).

**License key** (what it means for white-labeling): 🟢 permissive (MIT/Apache/BSD), rebrand and sell freely, keep notices · 🟡 weak copyleft (MPL/LGPL), share changes to *their* files · 🟠 GPL, fine for hosted SaaS, distributed apps must ship source · 🔴 AGPL, hosted users must be offered your modified source · ⚪ other: NOASSERTION means GitHub couldn't auto-detect it (often dual/custom/source-available), NONE means no license file, read it first.

**Fit** (1-5): how directly the project can become a business. 5 = host or sell almost as-is · 4 = strong product, needs packaging · 3 = tool/component, build around it · 2 = library/framework, sell services or hosting around it. 📦 = archived repo (fork it). Not legal advice; trademarks and logos are never licensed, so always rebrand.

## Auto Repair Shops

6 projects, sorted by fit then stars.

| Project | What it does | Customers | Business models | Niche ideas | Pricing · effort · gotchas |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **LubeLogger**<br>[hargata/lubelog](https://github.com/hargata/lubelog)<br>⭐ 3.0k · 🟢 MIT · App · Fit 3/5 | Vehicle maintenance and fuel mileage tracker<br>*Replaces:* Fleetio, Drivvo, Carfax Car Care | Car owners, small fleets | Managed hosting<br>Vertical SaaS<br>Mobile app on top | • Maintenance logs for small delivery fleets<br>• Garage tracker for car clubs<br>• Service tracking for taxis<br>*Angle:* Branded customer vehicle-maintenance portal for garages | $3-8/mo<br>*Effort:* Medium (Single-tenant, simple .NET)<br>⚠ MIT; LubeLogger name |
| **AndrOBD**<br>[fr3ts0n/AndrOBD](https://github.com/fr3ts0n/AndrOBD)<br>⭐ 2.2k · 🟠 GPL-3.0 · App · Fit 3/5 | Android app reading vehicle diagnostics through ELM327 OBD adapters.<br>*Replaces:* Torque Pro, OBD Fusion, Car Scanner, Autel apps | Car owners and independent mechanics | Mobile app on top<br>White-label reseller<br>Premium plugins/add-ons | • OBD scanner app for fleet drivers<br>• Diagnostics app for EV conversions<br>• Branded scan tool for garages<br>*Angle:* Shop-branded OBD diagnostic app for customers | $5-10 app or $15/mo garage plan<br>*Effort:* Medium (Android app, rebrand and Play store work)<br>⚠ GPL-3.0; distributed apps must ship source |
| **OCA Fleet**<br>[OCA/fleet](https://github.com/OCA/fleet)<br>⭐ 62 · 🔴 AGPL-3.0 · Platform · Fit 3/5 | Odoo modules for managing vehicle fleets.<br>*Replaces:* Fleetio, Samsara Fleet, Verizon Connect | Small companies with vehicle fleets | Setup + support retainer<br>Consulting/implementation<br>Managed hosting | • Fleet management for plumbing contractors<br>• Vehicle tracking for schools<br>• Fleet admin for NGOs<br>*Angle:* Fleet maintenance contracts for repair shops on Odoo | $5-10/vehicle/mo<br>*Effort:* Medium (Needs Odoo instance and customization)<br>⚠ AGPL-3.0; Odoo ecosystem versions |
| **python-OBD**<br>[brendan-w/python-OBD](https://github.com/brendan-w/python-OBD)<br>⭐ 1.3k · 🟠 GPL-2.0 · Library · Fit 2/5 | Python library for reading OBD-II engine data over ELM327 serial adapters.<br>*Replaces:* Torque Pro, OBD Fusion, Autel diagnostics | Developers building car diagnostics products | Setup + support retainer<br>Consulting/implementation<br>API/usage-based | • Fleet diagnostics logger for delivery vans<br>• Used-car health reports for dealers<br>• Driver coaching telematics for schools<br>*Angle:* Diagnostic kiosk for quick-lube shops | Custom builds $3-10k per project<br>*Effort:* Medium (Library only, needs UI and backend)<br>⚠ GPL-2.0; derivative works must stay GPL |
| **Automotive Repair Management**<br>[ChanMeng666/automotive-repair-management-system](https://github.com/ChanMeng666/automotive-repair-management-system)<br>⭐ 32 · 🟢 MIT · App · Fit 2/5 · 📦 archived | Flask and MySQL web app for managing automotive repair shops.<br>*Replaces:* Shopmonkey, Tekmetric, Mitchell 1 Manager SE | Small independent repair shops | Vertical SaaS<br>Setup + support retainer<br>Managed hosting | • Repair shop software for motorcycle garages<br>• Collision shop tracker<br>• Mobile mechanic job management<br>*Angle:* Work-order and invoice system for independent garages | $49-99/mo per shop<br>*Effort:* Medium (Single-tenant Flask app, small project)<br>⚠ MIT; limited maturity, no payments |
| **Auto Repair SaaS**<br>[wangonya/auto-repair-saas](https://github.com/wangonya/auto-repair-saas)<br>⭐ 18 · 🟢 Apache-2.0 · App · Fit 2/5 | Cloud auto repair shop management software.<br>*Replaces:* Shopmonkey, Tekmetric, Autoflow | Small repair shops | Vertical SaaS<br>Managed hosting<br>Setup + support retainer | • Repair shop SaaS for tire shops<br>• Detailing business job manager<br>• Mobile mechanic invoicing<br>*Angle:* Cloud shop management sold per bay | $39-79/mo per shop<br>*Effort:* Medium (Small codebase, needs multitenancy review)<br>⚠ Early-stage; few features |

| **Online Bakery Shop**<br>[odhiamboatieno/Online-Bakery-Shop-Management-System-Php](https://github.com/odhiamboatieno/Online-Bakery-Shop-Management-System-Php)<br>⭐ 23 · 🟠 GPL-3.0 · App · Fit 2/5 | PHP web shop where bakery owners sell products online and manage orders.<br>*Replaces:* Shopify, Square Online, Toast Online Ordering | Small bakeries | Setup + support retainer<br>Vertical SaaS<br>Agency service | • Online ordering for local bakeries<br>• Cake order manager<br>• Pickup ordering for patisseries<br>*Angle:* Pre-order website for neighborhood bakeries | $29/mo per bakery<br>*Effort:* Medium (Simple PHP app, single-tenant)<br>⚠ GPL-3.0; student-style project |
| **Prep Meals Traceability**<br>[linyshdhhcb/prep-meals-blockchain-traceability](https://github.com/linyshdhhcb/prep-meals-blockchain-traceability)<br>⭐ 7 · 🟢 MIT · Platform · Fit 2/5 | Spring Boot, Vue3 and WeChat mini-program for prepared meal traceability.<br>*Replaces:* FoodLogiQ, TraceGains, Trace One | Prepared-food producers in China | Consulting/implementation<br>Setup + support retainer<br>Vertical SaaS | • Traceability for central kitchens<br>• QR origin labels for food brands<br>• Ingredient audit for catering<br>*Angle:* Farm-to-table traceability for food manufacturers | $200-500/mo per producer<br>*Effort:* High (Chinese UI, blockchain stack, WeChat mini-program)<br>⚠ Food safety regulation; Chinese-language docs |

| **BEEP**<br>[beepnl/BEEP](https://github.com/beepnl/BEEP)<br>⭐ 130 · 🔴 AGPL-3.0 · Platform · Fit 4/5 | Bee monitoring app, API and measurement system for hive weight and sensor data<br>*Replaces:* Arnia, Hive-Tech, BroodMinder, Apis Protect | Beekeepers and beekeeping associations | Managed hosting<br>Hardware kits/assembly<br>Vertical SaaS | • Hive monitoring for hobby beekeeper clubs<br>• Apiary dashboards for honey producers<br>• Pollination tracking for orchard growers<br>*Angle:* Hive monitoring app and sensors for beekeeping associations | $5-15/mo per hive, $120 scale<br>*Effort:* Medium (Full stack, needs hosting and hardware supply)<br>⚠ AGPL-3.0 server changes must be published; BEEP name |
| **Hive Pal**<br>[martinhrvn/hive-pal](https://github.com/martinhrvn/hive-pal)<br>⭐ 38 · ⚪ NONE · App · Fit 3/5 | Mobile-first app to track beehives, inspections and colony health.<br>*Replaces:* Hive Tracks, BeeKeeper apps, Apiary Book | Beekeepers and apiary businesses | Managed hosting<br>Vertical SaaS<br>Mobile app on top | • Hive tracking for honey producers<br>• Apiary management for pollination services<br>• Beekeeping club hive registry<br>*Angle:* Hosted apiary inspection logbook, $5/mo per beekeeper | $5-15/mo per beekeeper<br>*Effort:* Medium (Web app, no license stated)<br>⚠ No license, ask author before commercial use |
| **Tanen**<br>[hmd83/tanen](https://github.com/hmd83/tanen)<br>⭐ 17 · 🟢 Apache-2.0 · Hardware · Fit 3/5 | LoRaWAN beehive monitor for weight, temperature and battery on nRF54 with 10 year battery life<br>*Replaces:* Arnia, BroodMinder, Hive-Tech | Commercial beekeepers with remote apiaries | Hardware kits/assembly<br>Managed hosting<br>Setup + support retainer | • Remote apiary monitoring via LoRaWAN<br>• Hive weight loggers for honey producers<br>• Long-life hive sensors for rural beekeepers<br>*Angle:* LoRaWAN hive scales for commercial pollinators | $90-160 per unit, $2/mo data<br>*Effort:* Medium (Needs LoRaWAN gateway coverage and hardware QA)<br>⚠ Apache-2.0; LoRa regional regulations |
| **BEEP Measurement System**<br>[beepnl/measurement-system-v3](https://github.com/beepnl/measurement-system-v3)<br>⭐ 22 · ⚪ NONE · Hardware · Fit 2/5 | BEEP base v3 steel-design hive measurement system hardware<br>*Replaces:* Arnia, BroodMinder, Hive-Tech | Beekeepers using BEEP platform | Hardware kits/assembly<br>White-label reseller<br>Setup + support retainer | • Hive scales for BEEP users<br>• Steel scale bases for commercial apiaries<br>• Weighing platforms for migratory beekeepers<br>*Angle:* Manufacture hive weight bases for apiaries | $150-300 per base<br>*Effort:* Medium (Hardware only, depends on BEEP backend)<br>⚠ No license stated; BEEP trademark |
